| 2026-10-25 | v6.99.13 | **Foundation & Navigation Architecture: Persistent Global Navigation with Contextual Submenus** — Refactored the Smart Navigation behaviour so opening a document or transaction never replaces, hides, or destroys the persistent global sidebar. Decoupled global navigation across all 7 authorized categories (Dashboard/Launchpad, Sales, Purchase, Inventory, Masters, Reports, System) from transaction-specific action sets. Nested contextual document actions (`doc-overview`, `doc-items`, `doc-stock`, `doc-verify`, `doc-dispatch`, `doc-print`, `doc-activity`) directly under the active workspace in `NavRail.tsx` and `NavRenderer.tsx`. Enforced dynamic lifecycle states (disabling `doc-dispatch` in `DRAFT`, disabling `doc-verify` in `POSTED`) and authoritative backend RBAC via `GET /api/v1/menus/contextual` and `security_matrix.py` (excluding Cashiers from `doc-dispatch`). Preserved full presentation parity across Regular UX, Classic UX, and mobile layouts with zero layout preference resets. Certified with 10/10 Vitest tests green (`persistentGlobalNavigationContextualSubmenus.test.tsx`), 43/43 tests green across affected suites, 7/7 backend Pytest tests green (`t_menu_registry.py`), and clean TypeScript `tsc --noEmit` (0 errors). | [Walkthrough](./foundation/Persistent_Global_Navigation_Contextual_Submenus_Walkthrough_v6.99.13.md) | [Plan](../implementation/foundation/Persistent_Global_Navigation_Contextual_Submenus_Plan_v1.0.0.md) | — | Done |

| 2026-10-23 | v6.99.13 | **Database & Systems: Automated Database Migration, Pre-Migration Backup & Child Customer Billing Remediation** — Delivered an enterprise-grade automated database migration, backup snapshot, and rollback engine (`scripts/auto_migrate.py`, `scripts/migrate.ps1`, `package.json`). Guarantees every migration session begins with a compressed `pg_dump -Fc` binary snapshot, SHA-256 manifest, and pre-migration row count census before executing Alembic head migrations across `smritisys` and tenant databases (`smriti001` through `smriti004`). Features automated rollback (`--auto-rollback`) and zero-data-loss verification. Diagnosed and resolved the 6 core architectural and configuration root causes preventing child customer billing in freshly installed versions: enabled parent visibility gate (`show_child_customers_in_billing`), mounted `SmritiCreditBillingTerminal` in `BillingWorkspace.tsx`, removed hardcoded mock SKUs (`SHOE-001`) that caused HTTP 400 errors, resolved Alembic migration `v1541` variant SKU uniqueness collision, calibrated A4 Tax Invoice background watermark to 75% page size, and validated live atomic checkout `INV-20261023-3CA746` in headless Playwright. Certified with 5/5 Vitest tests green (`childCustomerBillingVisibility.test.tsx`), zero syntax errors, and live PostgreSQL snapshot verification. | [Walkthrough](./db/DB_Automated_Migration_Pre_Backup_And_Child_Billing_Remediation_v6.99.13.md) | — | Done |
| 2026-10-23 | v6.99.14 | **CRM & Sales POS: End-to-End Customer Lifecycle Audit & Headless Verification** — Conducted rigorous headless end-to-end customer lifecycle audit across Customer Creation, Viewing, Editing, Optimistic Concurrency Locking, Billing/POS Selection, Parent-Controlled Child Customer Visibility, Sales Invoice Creation, and Invoice Retrieval. Discovered and fixed child customer checkout guard bypass in `headless_billing.py` (`calculate_billing`) by ensuring pre-resolved customers from `CanonicalSalesPostingWriter` are subject to child visibility policies. Fortified secondary PSV projection in `canonical_sales_writer.py` with nested savepoint isolation (`session.begin_nested()`) preventing non-fatal auxiliary projection exceptions from poisoning outer sales posting transactions. Aligned physical database schema on test database `smriti_test_phase2c` (`product_id` in `psv_sku_tracking`). Certified with 4/4 backend Pytest tests green (`test_customer_lifecycle_e2e.py`), 37/37 frontend Vitest tests green across customer suites, and zero syntax errors. | [Walkthrough](./crm/CRM_Customer_Lifecycle_End_To_End_Audit_v1.0.0.md) | — | Done |

| 2026-10-22 | v6.99.12 | **Foundation & UI Architecture: Classic UX Header, Submenu & Overflow Remediation** — Diagnosed and resolved root-cause navigation clipping, trapped dropdown menus, double-scrollbars, and sidebar/status bar collision in SMRITI Retail OS Classic UX mode. | 2026-10-22 | v6.99.11 | **Foundation & UI Architecture: Classic UX Workspace Directory & Traditional ERP Navigation Parity** — Transformed the SMRITI Retail OS Classic UX workspace directory from the previous card-based layout into a premium, traditional high-density ERP interface matching the approved visual specification. | 2026-10-22 | v3.22.0 | **Foundation & Disaster Recovery: Critical Backup Integrity Remediation & Full Database Recovery Engine** — Remediated critical backup integrity flaws where partial data exports (20.9 KB, only 1,000 product rows) were previously mislabeled and falsely certified as "Verified" and "Complete". Separated file integrity validation (cryptographic SHA-256 and Gzip decompression) from business completeness validation (verifying all required tables exist and are non-empty). Implemented PostgreSQL-native `pg_dump -Fc` producing ~14.5 MB recovery packages containing all 311 tables, 1,369 indexes, sequences, DDL schemas, and live records, verified via `pg_restore -l`. Repaired schema column mismatches against live PostgreSQL schema `smriti001` (`mobile` instead of `phone` on customers/suppliers, `grand_total` on sales_invoices, `stock_movements` for inventory). Isolated table extractions using nested savepoints (`sess.begin_nested()`), permanently eliminating the transaction abort cascade (`InFailedSQLTransactionError`). Enforced production restoration protection invariant permanently blocking incomplete/partial backups from overwriting live databases (`can_restore = False`). Redesigned `BackupRestoreWorkspace.tsx` completion card (560–680px responsive width, restrained 56px status icon, honest omitted tables warning callouts, and primary "View Backup History" / secondary "Back to Backup Centre" buttons). Certified with 14/14 backend Pytest tests green (`test_backup_integrity_remediation.py`, `test_master_backup_engine.py`, `test_backup_restore_service.py`), 10/10 frontend Vitest tests green (`backupRestoreWorkspace.test.tsx`), zero `tsc --noEmit` errors, and 100% table and row parity verified in isolated test database restores. | [Walkthrough](./foundation/Foundation_Critical_Backup_Integrity_Remediation_v3.22.0.md) | [Plan](../implementation/foundation/Foundation_Critical_Backup_Integrity_Remediation_Plan_v3.22.0.md) | Done |
| 2026-10-22 | v1.0.0 | **Foundation: Premium UX Switcher (Regular ↔ Classic) Architecture & Seamless Workspace Presentation** — Designed and delivered the authoritative Regular UX ↔ Classic UX Switcher in SMRITI Retail OS. Allows users to dynamically toggle between Regular UX (clean, spacious, guided everyday retail dashboard with welcome banner, 4 KPI cards, 4 action tiles, and recent sales) and Classic UX (traditional, high-efficiency, keyboard-driven ERP workspace with top menu bar, multi-tab strip, F-key toolbar F2-F10, and compact 10-column transactional POS grid). Built on decoupled presentation architecture sharing identical database models, tax computation pipelines, and active in-memory cart states—ensuring zero data loss when toggling modes during active POS checkout. Features top bar mode indicator pill ([ 💻 Regular UX ] / [ 💻 Classic UX ]) with 1-click toggling, user profile session dropdown with radio card selectors, and reusable UxSwitcherModal with side-by-side comparison. Backed by PostgreSQL layout preference persistence (/api/v1/system/layout/preferences) and zero-flash synchronous localStorage hydration. Certified with 5/5 Vitest tests green, 1/1 backend Pytest test green, and clean tsc --noEmit (0 errors). | [Walkthrough](./foundation/Premium_UX_Switcher_Regular_Classic_Walkthrough_v1.0.0.md) | [Plan](../implementation/foundation/Premium_UX_Switcher_Regular_Classic_Plan_v1.0.0.md) | Done |
| 2026-10-22 | v1.0.0 | **Foundation & Disaster Recovery: Master Backup & Restore Engine, Windows CLI & Integrity Verification** — Implemented an enterprise-grade disaster recovery system connecting the SMRITI browser interface directly to the Windows/Docker host execution engine. Eradicated all simulated progress, mock data, and unverified success statuses. Built `MasterBackupEngine` (`backup_engine.py`) with persistent disk JSON state (`backend/backups/jobs/{id}.json`) and persistent raw log streams (`backend/backups/logs/{id}.log`). Features automated startup crash reconciliation (`reconcile_interrupted_jobs`) converting orphaned in-flight jobs to `INTERRUPTED`, preflight storage validation with 2x safety margin and concurrent job mutual exclusion, regex-based secret redaction masking credentials and tokens, and atomic `.tmp` Gzip archive packaging with SHA-256 manifest verification. Built `BackupSchedulerDaemon` (`backup_scheduler.py`) running in FastAPI lifespan for periodic off-hours runs (Daily, Weekly, Monthly) and safe retention pruning with the invariant that the newest verified backup is unconditionally preserved. Created a native Windows PowerShell CLI suite in `scripts/`: `backup.ps1` (progress bar and live logs), `status.ps1` (protection and health inspector), `logs.ps1` (structured log tailer), and `restore.ps1` (guarded recovery with safety snapshots). Upgraded `BackupRestoreWorkspace.tsx` with a "Create System Backup?" confirmation modal, 8-stage live progress monitor, browser refresh reconnection via `GET /api/v1/backup/active-job`, and expandable real-time log terminal. Certified with 5/5 backend Pytest tests (`test_master_backup_engine.py`), 4/4 backend Pytest tests (`test_backup_restore_service.py`), 8/8 Vitest tests (`backupRestoreWorkspace.test.tsx`), and clean `tsc --noEmit` (0 errors). | [Walkthrough](./foundation/Master_Backup_Restore_Engine_And_Windows_CLI_Walkthrough_v1.0.0.md) | [Plan](../implementation/foundation/Backup_Scheduler_And_Retention_Pruning_Plan_v1.0.0.md) | Done |
| 2026-10-22 | v1.0.0 | **Operations & System: Premium Backup & Restore UX & Engine Implementation** — Designed and implemented a commercial-grade, non-technical Backup & Restore subsystem for SMRITI Retail OS (`BackupRestoreWorkspace.tsx`). Enables retail shop owners to back up business data, monitor multi-stage asynchronous job progress with circular progress ring and live 4-stage stepper, inspect verified recovery points, execute safe restorations guarded by explicit destructive overwrite warnings, and configure automatic schedules and storage destinations. Created backend `BackupRestoreService` and REST endpoints under `/api/v1/backup` (`status`, `history`, `create`, `jobs/{id}`, `cancel`, `restore/review`, `restore/execute`, `settings`) with SHA-256 tamper-evident manifest verification, gzip compression, and pre-restore safety snapshots. Registered in Launchpad under "System & Operations", added to workspace registry, and routed in `TabRenderer.tsx`. Certified with 4/4 backend Pytest tests (`test_backup_restore_service.py`), 8/8 Vitest tests (`backupRestoreWorkspace.test.tsx`), 11/11 launchpad tests, clean `tsc --noEmit` (0 errors), and live database verification. | [Walkthrough](./foundation/Premium_Backup_Restore_UX_Walkthrough_v1.0.0.md) | [Plan](../implementation/foundation/Premium_Backup_Restore_UX_Plan_v1.0.0.md) | Done |
| 2026-10-22 | v1.0.0 | **Reports & BI: Report Designer Intelligent Auto-Consolidation & Dynamic Grouping Engine** — Implemented server-side intelligent row auto-consolidation and dynamic multi-dimensional grouping within SMRITI BI & Reporting Center Report Builder (`ReportBuilder.tsx`). Created centralized semantic field metadata registry (`ReportFieldRegistry` in `report_field_registry.py`) classifying attributes into Grouping Dimensions (Supplier, PO No, Article, Color, Size, Branch, Date), Additive Measures (`SUM` for Quantity, Net Amount, Tax Amount), Non-Additive Measures (`AVG` for rates, unit prices), and Record Counts. Upgraded `ReportsService.query_report_builder` to dynamically construct SQL `GROUP BY` expressions based on selected columns, collapsing identical dimensions into single consolidated rows with exact sums while automatically splitting groups when granular dimensions (e.g., Size, Color) are toggled. Eliminated Cartesian join inflation on 1:N purchase bills via correlated scalar subquery. Added frontend Row Consolidation toolbar controls (`Auto`, `Detailed Rows`, `Group By`), consolidated row count badges (`N lines`), and interactive drill-down drawer. Certified with 3/3 backend Pytest tests (`test_report_builder_auto_consolidation.py`), 5/5 Vitest tests (`reportBuilderAutoConsolidation.test.ts`), 20/20 regression tests, clean `tsc --noEmit` (0 errors), and live PostgreSQL database verification. | [Walkthrough](./reports/Report_Designer_Auto_Consolidation_Walkthrough_v1.0.0.md) | [Plan](../implementation/reports/Report_Designer_Auto_Consolidation_Plan_v1.0.0.md) | Done |

| 2026-10-21 | v1.0.0 | **Inventory & Stock Ledger: Single-Door Stock Ledger Architecture, Dual-Hash Idempotency, Deadlock-Free Canonical Lock Ordering & AST Guard** — Engineered and deployed the authoritative Single-Door Stock Ledger Architecture (`StockLedger.post()` / `StockLedger.post_many()`) establishing absolute transaction convergence across SMRITI Retail OS. Implemented database migration `v1542_stock_ledger_idempotency_and_single_door.py` introducing `idempotency_key`, `payload_hash`, `cost_source`, and partial unique index `uq_sm_company_idempotency_key` (`WHERE idempotency_key IS NOT NULL`). Enforces SHA-256 idempotency key deduplication and SHA-256 payload hash verification raising HTTP 409 Conflict on payload drift. Engineered canonical 3-tier lock hierarchy (`Product` -> `ProductBatchStock` -> `Warehouse` tenant validation) globally sorted by composite key `(product_id, batch_no, warehouse_id)` ensuring guaranteed deadlock-free execution under concurrent multi-terminal bursts. Built nested savepoint-guarded insertion (`session.begin_nested()`) with graceful race recovery. Decoupled `StockSynchronizer` from mutation side effects, converting it into a pure recompute-and-write-cache service with `HEALTH_FINDING_UNLEDGERED_OPENING_STOCK` logging, establishing explicit `StockLedger.post_admin_opening_stock()` for administrative opening balances. Fortified valuation boundary with typed `InternalCost` dataclass and Pydantic validator rejecting client-supplied `unit_cost` with HTTP 422. Migrated all callers across `inventory.py`, `sales_stock_authority.py`, `goods_receipt.py`, `stock_acct_svc.py`, `ecom_engine.py`, and `stock_audit_service.py`. Enforced AST static analysis guard `scripts/ci_stock_single_door_guard.py` scanning 763 files across `backend/app/` with 0 violations. Certified with 16/16 backend tests green across `test_single_door_stock_ledger.py`, `test_single_door_ast_guard.py`, and `test_phase0_synchronizer_characterization.py`. | [Walkthrough](./inventory/Single_Door_Stock_Ledger_Architecture_Walkthrough_v1.0.0.md) | [Plan](../implementation/inventory/Single_Door_Stock_Ledger_Architecture_Plan_v1.0.0.md) | Done |

| 2026-10-19 | v6.99.0 | **CRM & Customer Master: Customer Master CRUD Lifecycle UX, Concurrency & Safety Architecture** — Implemented an enterprise-grade Customer Master lifecycle with distinct Create, View, Edit, Deactivate/Reactivate, and conditionally permitted Permanent Delete workflows in SMRITI Retail OS. Features automated pre-flight foreign key dependency analysis querying 8 accounting and relational tables (`SalesInvoice`, `SalesOrder`, `SalesReturn`, `CustomerCreditLedger`, `CustomerPurchaseOrder`, `PSVParty`, `CustomerRelationship`, and outstanding balance) via `GET /crm/customers/{id}/delete-eligibility`. Integrated real-time debounced duplicate candidate detection (`/crm/customers/check-duplicate`) in `CreateCustomerModal.tsx` supporting Retail Fast Path vs Corporate B2B with server-side customer code generation. | 2026-10-02 | v6.51.0 | **Procurement & Finance: SMRITI Procurement Phase 2.9 Ã¢â‚¬â€ Statutory Withholding Tax (TDS on Purchase & Payments Ã¢â‚¬â€ Section 194Q / 194C / 194J) & GL Account 2030 Integration** Ã¢â‚¬â€ Implemented comprehensive Indian Statutory Withholding Tax (TDS) engine under the Income Tax Act, 1961, across procurement bills and supplier payments; built `StatutoryTdsEngine` with strict PAN regex validation, 4th character entity classification (Company vs Individual vs HUF vs Firm), standard rates (194Q: 0.10%, 194C: 1%/2%, 194J: 2%/10%, 194H: 5%), and Section 206AA penal rate enforcement (5% / 20%); registered `Account 2030: TDS / Withholding Tax Payable` under Current Liabilities (`2000`) in `DEFAULT_CHART_OF_ACCOUNTS` and company DB seeding; extended `UnifiedAccountingLedgerService` with balanced, symmetrical double-entry postings & reversals (`DR 1040/GST = CR 2010 Net AP + CR 2030 TDS` for bills; `DR 2010 Gross Settled = CR 1010/1020 Net Cash + CR 2030 TDS` for payments; `DR 2010 = CR 2030` for standalone TDS vouchers); added REST APIs `POST /api/v1/vendors/tds/calculate` and `GET /api/v1/vendors/{vendor_id}/tds-summary`; integrated 4-column financial summary ribbon in `VendorPayablesTab.tsx` and `StandaloneVendorPayablesPreview.tsx` showing Account 2030 Statutory TDS Withheld, active statutory rate badges, and PAN validation status; captured 2 headless Playwright visual evidence screenshots; verified with 10/10 automated pytest tests green in `test_statutory_tds_engine.py` and zero TypeScript compiler errors (`tsc --noEmit` exit 0). | [Walkthrough](./procurement/Procurement_Phase2_9_Statutory_TDS_Withholding_Tax_v1.0.md) | [Plan](../implementation/procurement/Procurement_Phase2_9_Statutory_TDS_Withholding_Tax_Plan_v1.0.md) | Done |
